Conclusion: 初始噪声控制可作为3D补全中独立于采样轨迹操纵的有效新维度，无需额外训练即可实现高质量的3D补全。

Abstract: We present a training-free approach for controllable 3D inpainting based on initial noise optimization. In the structured 3D latent diffusion framework, we observe that the underlying geometric structure is established during the early stages of the diffusion process and exhibits high sensitivity to the initial noise. Such characteristics compromise stability in tasks like inpainting and editing, where the model must ensure strict alignment with the existing context while synthesizing a new structure. In this paper, we introduce a strategy to optimize the initial noise within the structured 3D latent diffusion framework, ensuring high-fidelity 3D inpainting. Specifically, we update the initial noise by leveraging a backpropagation approximation grounded in the rectified flow model, with the spectral parameterization specially designed for robust and efficient structured 3D latent optimization. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in contextual consistency and prompt alignment over representative training-free inpainting baselines, establishing initial noise control as an independent dimension for 3D inpainting, orthogonal to conventional sampling trajectory manipulation.

</details>


### [37] [Prediction of Alzheimer's Disease Risk Factors from Retinal Images via Deep Learning: Development and Validation of Biologically Relevant Morphological Associations in the UK Biobank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00665)
*Seowung Leem,Yunchao Yang,Adam J. Woods,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究利用深度学习模型从彩色眼底照片(CFP)中预测12个与阿尔茨海默病(AD)相关的风险因素（如年龄、性别、吸烟、饮酒、BMI、血压、HbA1c等），发现模型性能优于传统形态计量学方法，且基于显著性得分的视网膜特征在AD发病前平均8.55年即与对照组存在显著差异，表明CFP可能编码与AD易感性相关的视网膜结构变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统性、代谢性和生活方式因素已通过流行病学和AD特异性生物标志物研究被证实与AD相关，但彩色眼底照片(CFP)是否包含对应这些AD风险领域的视网膜结构特征尚不清楚。本研究旨在探究深度学习模型能否从CFP中预测AD相关风险因素，并表征这些预测背后的视网膜结构，从而评估CFP是否反映AD易感性通路。

Method: 使用UK Biobank数据集中的62,876张CFP图像（来自44,501名参与者），训练深度学习模型预测6个分类变量（性别、吸烟、失眠、经济状况、饮酒、抑郁）和6个连续变量（年龄、受教育年限、BMI、收缩压、舒张压、HbA1c）。评估模型性能、模型显著性图及基于显著性的CAM-Score，并与视网膜形态测量结果进行比较。还将这些分数在AD发病前平均8.55年的病例组与匹配对照组之间进行比较。

Result: 深度学习模型对分类变量的AUROC范围为0.5654-0.9480，对连续变量的R²范围为-0.0291-0.7620，性能优于大多数形态计量学-机器学习模型。基于显著性的得分一致突出生物学上有意义的区域，特别是视盘和视网膜血管，并与现有的形态测量变异一致。多个显著性得分在AD病例与对照组之间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: CFP编码了与AD风险因素相关的视网膜特征。尽管不具有诊断性，但DL衍生的视网膜表征可能揭示反映AD潜在易感性的、有生物学意义的风险相关结构变化。

Abstract: The systemic, metabolic, lifestyle factors have established associations with Alzheimer's Disease (AD) through epidemiologic and AD-specific biomarker studies. Whether colored fundus photography (CFP) contains retinal structural signatures corresponding to these AD-related risk domains remains unclear. To determine whether deep learning (DL) models can predict 12 AD-related risk factors from CFP and to characterize the retinal structures underlying these predictions, thereby assessing whether CFP reflects pathways to AD vulnerability. Using UK Biobank CFPs, DL models were trained using 62,876 images from 44,501 unique participants to predict 12 factors linked to AD incidence: 6 categorical (sex, smoking, sleeplessness, economic status, alcohol use, depression) and 6 continuous (age, age at completing education, BMI, systolic, diastolic blood pressure, HbA1c). Model performance, model saliency, and saliency-derived scores (CAM-Score) were evaluated and compared to retinal morphometry. The scores were also compared between incident-AD cases (average 8.55 years before onset) and matched controls. Performance of DL ranged from AUROC= 0.5654-0.9480 for categorical and R2=-0.0291-0.7620 for continuous factors, outperforming most of the morphometry-machine learning models. Saliency-based score consistently highlighted biologically meaningful regions, particularly the optic nerve head and retinal vasculature. It also aligned with present morphometric variations. Several saliency-based scores differed significantly between incident AD and matched controls, suggesting potential overlap between retinal correlates of risk factors and preclinical AD-associated changes. CFP encodes retinal signatures linked to AD risk factors. Although not diagnostic, DL-derived retinal representations may uncover biologically meaningful risk-related structural changes mirroring the potential AD vulnerability.

</details>


### [38] [DMDSC: A Dynamic-Margin Deep Simplex Classifier for Open-Set Recognition on Medical Image Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00675)
*Vishal,Arnav Aditya,Nitin Kumar,Saurabh J. Shigwan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对医疗影像数据集中的极端类别不平衡问题，本文提出DMDSC框架，通过动态边界机制根据类别频率自适应调整分类边界，对稀有病理类别施加更高惩罚和更紧密的特征聚类，在多个医疗基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗影像数据集存在极端类别不平衡（罕见病理样本远少于常见疾病），这对开放集识别（OSR）构成双重挑战：既要保持已知类的高分类精度，又要可靠地拒绝训练中未见的未知样本。现有的DSC和UCDSC方法虽利用神经坍缩（Neural Collapse）实现最大类间间隔，但采用统一边界，未考虑医疗类别间密度的差异。

Method: 提出DMDSC（Dynamic Margin Deep Simplex Classifier）框架，引入动态边界（dynamic margin）方法：基于类别标签频率自动调整类特定边界，对稀有病理类别施加更高惩罚并强制更紧密的特征聚类，以抵消数据不平衡带来的负面影响。

Result: 在BloodMNIST、OCTMNIST、DermaMNIST和BreaKHis四个医疗基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，DMDSC框架优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 动态边界机制能有效应对医疗影像中的类别不平衡问题，提升开放集识别性能，为不平衡医疗数据下的OSR提供了一种改进方案。

Abstract: Medical imaging datasets are often characterized by extreme class imbalances, where rare pathologies are significantly underrepresented compared to common conditions. This imbalance poses a dual challenge for Open-Set Recognition (OSR): models must maintain high classification accuracy on known classes while reliably rejecting unknown samples unseen during training in the clinical settings. While recently proposed Deep Simplex Classifier (DSC)~\cite{cevikalp2024reaching} and UnCertainty-aware Deep Simplex Classifier (UCDSC)~\cite{Aditya_2026_WACV} successfully leverage Neural Collapse to ensure maximal inter-class separation, they rely on a uniform margin that does not account for the varying densities of medical classes.
  In this paper, we propose DMDSC an enhanced framework featuring a dynamic margin approach. Our approach automatically adapts class-specific margins based on label frequency, enforcing a higher penalty and tighter feature clustering for rare pathologies to counteract the effects of data imbalance. Extensive experiments conducted on diverse medical benchmarks on BloodMNIST\cite{medmnistv2}, OCTMNIST\cite{medmnistv2}, DermaMNIST\cite{medmnistv2}, and BreaKHis~\cite{spanhol2015dataset} datasets, demonstrate that our framework outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [39] [Foundation AI Models for Aerosol Optical Depth Estimation from PACE Satellite Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00678)
*Zahid Hassan Tushar,Sanjay Purushotham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ViTCG框架，基于视觉Transformer和通道分组空间回归方法，利用高光谱遥感影像进行气溶胶光学厚度（AOD）反演。相比传统物理方法和现有基础模型（如Prithvi），在PACE卫星数据上均方误差降低62%，且生成空间一致的AOD场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AOD反演方法存在两大问题：一是传统物理方法依赖辐射传输模型、查找表和辅助气象数据，计算开销大；二是现有数据驱动方法未能充分利用高光谱影像的空间-光谱一致性，导致反演结果空间不连续且对噪声敏感。

Method: 提出ViTCG（Vision Transformer with Channel-wise Grouping），以高光谱大气顶层辐射（TOA radiance）为输入，通过通道分组机制联合建模空间上下文和光谱信息，实现空间回归的AOD反演。

Result: 基于PACE卫星辐射观测数据，ViTCG相比包括Prithvi在内的最先进基础模型，均方误差（MSE）降低62%，并生成空间一致的AOD场。

Conclusion: 首次探索将Foundation AI模型用于AOD反演，ViTCG通过联合空间-光谱建模有效降低了反演偏差和误差，验证了基础模型在地球物理参数反演中的潜力。

Abstract: Aerosol Optical Depth (AOD) retrieval is essential for Earth observation, supporting applications from air quality monitoring to climate studies. Conventional physics-based AOD retrieval methods formulate the problem as a pixel-wise inversion, relying on radiative transfer modeling, memory-intensive look-up tables, and auxiliary meteorological data. While recent data-driven approaches have shown promise, many fail to exploit the spatial-spectral coherence of hyperspectral imagery, leading to spatially inconsistent and noise-sensitive retrievals. We present the first study exploring Foundation AI models for AOD retrieval and propose ViTCG, a Vision Transformer with Channel-wise Grouping-based spatial regression framework that reduces retrieval bias and error. ViTCG uses hyperspectral top-of-atmosphere radiance as input and jointly models spatial context and spectral information. Validation with PACE radiance observations demonstrates a 62% reduction in mean squared error compared to state-of-the-art foundation models, including Prithvi, and produces spatially coherent AOD fields.

</details>


### [40] [PhysEdit: Physically-Consistent Region-Aware Image Editing via Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00707)
*Guandong Li,Mengxia Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysEdit提出了一种自适应推理框架，通过复杂度自适应推理深度（CARD）和空间推理掩码（SRM）两个无需重训练的推理模块，根据指令动态调整推理步数和空间范围，在保持编辑质量的同时实现1.18倍加速，最高达1.52倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理驱动图像编辑器对所有指令使用统一的固定推理策略，但不同编辑指令（如颜色替换、物体插入、物理动作编辑）需要不同的空间覆盖和推理深度，缺乏适应性。

Method: 提出PhysEdit框架，包含两个推理模块：(1) CARD模块，根据指令和参考图像预测编辑复杂度，自适应分配推理步数N_r和推理token长度r；(2) SRM模块，从交叉注意力中提取指令条件化的空间先验，将推理约束在语义相关的区域。

Result: 在737样本的ImgEdit基准上，PhysEdit相比强推理基线实现1.18倍加速（每样本64.3s vs 76.1s），指令遵循度提升0.7%（CLIP-T 0.2283 vs 0.2266），身份保持基本持平（CLIP-I 0.8246 vs 0.8280）。外观级编辑加速达1.52倍。

Conclusion: 验证了沿空间和时间两个轴的自适应性是提升推理驱动图像编辑效率的关键维度，CARD的自适应分配是效率提升的主要来源。

Abstract: Image editing instructions are heterogeneous: a color swap, an object insertion, and a physical-action edit all demand different spatial coverage and different reasoning depth, yet existing reasoning-based editors apply a single fixed inference recipe to every instruction. We argue that adaptivity along both the spatial and temporal axes is the missing degree of freedom, and we present PhysEdit, an editing framework built around this principle. PhysEdit introduces two inference-time modules that compose without retraining the backbone. At its core, (1) Complexity-Adaptive Reasoning Depth (CARD) predicts edit complexity directly from the instruction and reference image and allocates the reasoning step count N_r and reasoning-token length r per sample -- turning a previously fixed inference schedule into a conditional-computation problem. CARD is supported by (2) a Spatial Reasoning Mask (SRM) that extracts an instruction-conditioned spatial prior from cross-attention to confine reasoning to regions that semantically require it. On the full 737-case ImgEdit Basic-Edit Suite, PhysEdit delivers a 1.18x wall-clock speedup (64.3s vs. 76.1s per sample) over a strong reasoning baseline while slightly improving instruction adherence (CLIP-T 0.2283 vs. 0.2266, +0.7%) and matching identity preservation within noise (CLIP-I 0.8246 vs. 0.8280). The speedup is category-dependent and reaches 1.52x on appearance-level edits, validating CARD's adaptive allocation as the principal source of efficiency gain. A 30-sample pilot with full ablations isolates the contribution of each module.

</details>


### [41] [Exploring the Limits of End-to-End Feature-Affinity Propagation for Single-Point Supervised Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00722)
*Qiancheng Zhou,Wenhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GSACP，一种基于批内点锚定特征亲和传播的端到端单点监督红外小目标检测方法，无需外部伪标签迭代。通过理论分析自参考传播漂移问题，并设计消融实验解耦优化瓶颈，在SIRST3数据集上以0.6674 mIoU和比PAL降低38%的虚警率实现了超低虚警工作模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单点监督IRSTD方法依赖显式、离线的伪标签构建（如多阶段主动学习、物理驱动掩码生成），流程复杂且成本高。本文探索一种极简替代方案：通过批内点锚定特征亲和传播在线生成点到掩码的监督信号，完全消除外部标签演化循环，实现端到端训练。

Method: 提出GSACP框架：1) 基于硬间隔特征亲和力（由局部图像先验门控）在线传播点锚定特征，直接监督检测器；2) 理论形式化自参考传播漂移问题（表示-监督纠缠）；3) 设计协议化单变量消融流程，包括局部EMA教师解耦、硬背景对比分离、自适应支持几何三种策略来隔离失效模式。

Result: 在SIRST3数据集上，GSACP-Final达到0.6674 mIoU，虚警率（Fa）相比PAL降低38%，建立了超低虚警工作模式。

Conclusion: 端到端的批内特征传播为虚警抑制至关重要的部署场景提供了一种紧凑替代方案。通过系统解构该范式，本文绘制了其性能边界。

Abstract: Single-point supervised infrared small target detection (IRSTD) drastically reduces dense annotation costs. Current state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods achieve high precision by recovering mask supervision through explicit, offline pseudo-label construction, such as multi-stage active learning and physics-driven mask generation. In this paper, we study a minimalist alternative: generating point-to-mask supervision online through in-batch, point-anchored feature-affinity propagation. We instantiate this paradigm as GSACP, an end-to-end testbed that directly supervises the detector using hard-margin feature affinity gated by local image priors, entirely eliminating external label-evolution loops.
  This compact design, however, exposes an optimization bottleneck. Because the affinity target is generated from the same feature representation being optimized, training forms a self-referential loop. We theoretically formalize this as \emph{Self-Referential Propagation Drift}, a representation-supervision entanglement that can sharpen true boundaries or distort the feature space to satisfy its own targets. To systematically isolate these failure modes, we apply a protocolized single-variable ablation procedure spanning local EMA teacher decoupling, hard-background contrastive separation, and adaptive support geometry.
  On the SIRST3 dataset, GSACP-Final establishes a new ultra-low false-alarm operating regime, achieving a highly competitive $0.6674$ mIoU while demonstrating a $38\% relative reduction in false-positive artifacts ($\mathrm{Fa}$) compared with PAL. By systematically deconstructing the end-to-end paradigm, we map its performance boundaries and show that in-batch feature propagation provides a compact alternative for deployment scenarios where false-alarm suppression is paramount.

</details>


### [42] [Quantum Gradient-Based Approach for Edge and Corner Detection Using Sobel Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00744)
*Mohammad Aamir Sohail,Gabriela Pinheiro,Yasemin Poyraz Kocak,Batuhan Hangun,Emre Camkerten,Simge Yigit,Hafize Asude Ertan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于Sobel边缘检测和Harris角点检测的量子实现方案，采用FRQI和QPIE两种量子图像编码方法进行对比，提出了基于lag-2差分的量子梯度计算方案，并引入经典后处理步骤减少误检。实验表明QPIE配置在有限测量次数下比FRQI更稳定，但整体成本仍受限于状态制备、测量和经典后处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子计算在经典图像处理任务（边缘检测和角点检测）中的可行性与实现方案，对比不同量子图像编码方法的性能差异，为量子图像处理提供功能性验证。

Method: 采用FRQI和QPIE两种量子图像编码方法对输入数据进行编码；提出基于lag-2差分的量子梯度计算方案，在叠加态中评估类梯度特征；对量子电路识别出的候选角点进行经典后处理以减少误检。

Result: 量子电路的输出与经典Sobel和Harris算子结果一致；QPIE配置比FRQI更稳定和连贯，尤其在有限测量次数下；梯度计算在电路层面高效，但总体成本由状态制备、测量和经典后处理主导。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了经典边缘检测和角点检测方法的量子化功能验证与可扩展实现，但并非端到端的加速方案；在无噪声模拟下完成所有实验，NISQ硬件上的性能可能受噪声和测量限制影响。

Abstract: Edge detection refers to identifying points in a digital image where intensity changes sharply, indicating object boundaries or structural features. Corners are locations where gray-level intensity changes abruptly in multiple directions and are widely used in feature extraction, object tracking, and 3D modeling. In this study, we present a quantum implementation of Sobel-based edge detection and Harris-style corner detection. Two quantum image encoding methods - Flexible Representation of Quantum Images (FRQI) and Quantum Probability Image Encoding (QPIE) - are used to encode the input data and are comparatively analyzed. The proposed approach introduces a quantum gradient computation scheme based on lag-2 differences, enabling the evaluation of gradient-like features in superposition. To improve detection quality and reduce false positives, a classical post-processing step is applied to candidate corner points identified by the quantum circuit. Results show that the proposed quantum circuits produce outputs consistent with classical Sobel and Harris operators. Furthermore, the QPIE-based configuration yields more stable and coherent results than FRQI, especially under limited measurement shots. While gradient computation can be performed efficiently at the circuit level, the overall cost remains dominated by state preparation, measurement, and classical post-processing. All experiments are conducted under noiseless simulation, and performance on NISQ hardware may be affected by noise and measurement limitations. Therefore, this work demonstrates a functional and scalable quantum realization of classical edge and corner detection methods rather than an end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [43] [Make Your LVLM KV Cache More Lightweight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00789)
*Xihao Chen,Yangyang Guo,Roger Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightKV通过利用视觉token嵌入中的冗余性，在预填充阶段采用跨模态消息传递和文本提示引导来压缩KV缓存，仅用55%的原始视觉token即可将视觉token KV缓存减半、计算量减少40%，同时保持通用性能并显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在推理时采用了KV缓存，但大量视觉token在预填充阶段会产生显著的GPU内存开销，直接沿用LLM中的KV缓存方法效率低下。

Method: LightKV提出利用视觉token嵌入间的冗余性，通过文本提示引导的跨模态消息传递，在预填充阶段聚合信息性消息并渐进压缩视觉token，实现与纯视觉压缩策略不同的提示感知压缩。

Result: 在8个开源LVLM和8个公开基准数据集（如MME、SeedBench）上，仅用55%的原始视觉token，即可将视觉token KV缓存减半、计算量减少40%，同时保持通用性能并显著优于现有基线。

Conclusion: LightKV是一种有效的LVLM KV缓存压缩方法，通过文本提示引导的跨模态压缩策略在降低内存开销和计算成本的同时保持模型性能，优于现有纯视觉压缩方法。

Abstract: Key-Value (KV) cache has become a de facto component of modern Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) for inference. While it enhances decoding efficiency in Large Language Models (LLMs), its direct adoption in LVLMs introduces substantial GPU memory overhead due to the large number of vision tokens processed during the prefill stage. To tackle this problem, we propose LightKV, a novel approach that reduces KV cache size by exploiting the redundancy among vision-token embeddings. Guided by text prompts, LightKV employs cross-modality message passing to aggregate informative messages across vision tokens and progressively compress them during prefill. This prompt-aware guidance distinguishes our method from prior vision-only compression strategies. We evaluate LightKV on eight open-source LVLMs across eight public benchmark datasets, e.g., MME and SeedBench. Experimental results demonstrate that with only 55% of the original vision tokens, LightKV (a) halves the vision-token KV cache size, (b) reduces computation by up to 40%, and (c) preserves general-purpose performance while significantly outperforming existing baselines.

</details>


### [44] [Modeling Subjective Urban Perception with Human Gaze](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00764)
*Lin Che,Xi Wang,Marc Pollefeys,Konrad Schindler,Martin Raubal,Peter Kiefer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Place Pulse-Gaze数据集，将街景图像与眼动追踪和主观感知标签结合，并设计了基于注视引导的城市感知框架，研究表明注视行为对建模主观城市感知具有重要价值，融合注视与场景表示可提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算模型直接从街景图像建模城市感知，忽视了人类感知的主观形成过程（即注视行为在其中所起的作用）。

Method: 构建Place Pulse-Gaze数据集（街景图像+眼动追踪+主观感知标签），提出Gaze-Guided Urban Perception Framework，包含三种设置：仅注视建模、注视融合显式语义场景表示、注视融合隐式丰富视觉表示。

Result: 注视信号本身已能有效预测主观城市感知；将注视与语义/丰富视觉表示融合可进一步提升预测性能。

Conclusion: 融入人类感知过程对城市场景理解至关重要，为注视引导的多模态城市计算开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Urban perception describes how people subjectively evaluate urban environments, shaping how cities are experienced and understood. Existing computational approaches primarily model urban perception directly from street view images, but largely ignore the human perceptual process through which such judgments are formed. In this paper, we introduce Place Pulse-Gaze, an urban perception dataset that augments street view images with synchronized eye-tracking recordings and individual perception labels. Based on this dataset, we propose a Gaze-Guided Urban Perception Framework to study how gaze behavior contributes to the modeling of subjective urban perception. The framework systematically investigates three complementary settings: gaze-only modeling, gaze fusion with explicit semantic scene representations, and gaze fusion with implicit richer visual representations. Experiments show that gaze alone already carries useful predictive signals for subjective urban perception, and that integrating gaze with scene representations further improves prediction under both semantic and richer visual representations. Overall, our findings highlight the importance of incorporating human perceptual processes into urban scene understanding and open a direction for gaze-guided multimodal urban computing.

</details>


### [45] [GMGaze: MoE-Based Context-Aware Gaze Estimation with CLIP and Multiscale Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00799)
*Xinyuan Zhao,Yihang Wu,Ahmad Chaddad,Sarah A. Alkhodair,Reem Kateb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GMGaze，一种基于多尺度Transformer的全局条件化注视估计方法，通过语义原型条件、早期统一融合和稀疏MoE模块，在四个公开基准上取得SOTA性能，跨域评估也表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CNN、Transformer和CLIP的注视估计方法存在三大挑战：图像特征晚期融合导致信息丢失、缺乏因子感知的条件化、以及容量扩展不切实际。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 提出GMGaze模型：1) 语义原型条件模块，利用四个可学习原型库（光照、背景、头部姿态、外观）调控CLIP全局图像嵌入，生成两个互补的上下文偏置全局token；2) 早期统一融合，将全局token、CLIP patch token和CNN token在第一层融合；3) 稀疏MoE模块，提供条件计算容量；4) 对抗域适应技术配合特征分离损失，使两个全局token保持去相关。

Result: 在MPIIFaceGaze、EYEDIAP、Gaze360和ETH-XGaze四个基准上分别达到2.49°、3.22°、10.16°和1.44°的平均角度误差，在所有域内设置中均优于先前基线。跨域评估在两个标准迁移路线上取得SOTA结果。

Conclusion: GMGaze通过早期统一融合和语义原型条件有效解决了现有方法的局限性，在域内和跨域注视估计任务中均实现了领先性能。

Abstract: Gaze estimation methods commonly use facial appearances to predict the direction of a person gaze. However, previous studies show three major challenges with convolutional neural network (CNN)-based, transformer-based, and contrastive language-image pre-training (CLIP)-based methods, including late fusion of image features, lack of factor-aware conditioning, and impractical capacity scaling. To address these challenges, we propose Globally-conditioned Multi-scale Gaze estimation (GMGaze), which leverages a multi-scale transformer architecture. Specifically, the model first introduces semantic prototype conditioning, which modulates the CLIP global image embedding using four learned prototype banks (i.e., illumination, background, head pose and appearance) to generate two complementary context-biased global tokens. These tokens, along with the CLIP patch and CNN tokens, are fused at the first layer. This early unified fusion prevents information loss common in late-stage merging. Finally, each token passes through sparse Mixture-of-Experts modules, providing conditional computational capacity without uniformly increasing dense parameters. For cross-domain adaptation, we incorporate an adversarial domain adaptation technique with a feature separation loss that encourages the two global tokens to remain de-correlated. Experiments using four public benchmarks (MPIIFaceGaze, EYEDIAP, Gaze360, and ETH-XGaze) show that GMGaze achieves mean angular errors of 2.49$^\circ$, 3.22$^\circ$, 10.16$^\circ$, and 1.44$^\circ$, respectively, outperforming previous baselines in all within-domain settings. In cross-domain evaluations, it provides state-of-the-art (SOTA) results on two standard transfer routes.

</details>


### [46] [Let ViT Speak: Generative Language-Image Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00809)
*Yan Fang,Mengcheng Lan,Zilong Huang,Weixian Lei,Yunqing Zhao,Yujie Zhong,Yingchen Yu,Qi She,Yao Zhao,Yunchao Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenLIP是一种用于多模态大语言模型的Vision Transformer最小化生成式预训练框架，通过直接让ViT从视觉token预测语言token，无需对比学习批次构建或额外文本解码器，在8B样本上训练后达到或超越强基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地将视觉编码器与LLM的自回归性质对齐，提出一种更简单、可扩展且高性能的视觉-语言联合预训练方法。

Method: 使用标准语言建模目标，训练ViT直接从视觉token预测语言token，采用单一transformer联合建模视觉和文本token，无需对比学习批次构建或额外文本解码器。

Result: 在8B样本的Recap-DataComp-1B上训练后，GenLIP在多种多模态基准上匹配或超越使用更多预训练数据的强基线；在多分辨率、原始宽高比的图像上继续预训练后，在OCR和图表理解等细节敏感任务上进一步提升。

Conclusion: GenLIP提供了一种简洁、可扩展且有效的视觉编码器预训练方案，可作为MLLM中视觉编码器的强基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we present \textbf{Gen}erative \textbf{L}anguage-\textbf{I}mage \textbf{P}re-training (GenLIP), a minimalist generative pretraining framework for Vision Transformers (ViTs) designed for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). To better align vision encoders with the autoregressive nature of LLMs, GenLIP trains a ViT to predict language tokens directly from visual tokens using a standard language modeling objective, without contrastive batch construction or an additional text decoder. This design offers three key advantages: (1) \textbf{Simplicity}: a single transformer jointly models visual and textual tokens; (2) \textbf{Scalability}: it scales effectively with both data and model size; and (3) \textbf{Performance}: it achieves competitive or superior results across diverse multimodal benchmarks. Trained on 8B samples from Recap-DataComp-1B, GenLIP matches or surpasses strong baselines despite using substantially less pretraining data. After continued pretraining on multi-resolution images at native aspect ratios, GenLIP further improves on detail-sensitive tasks such as OCR and chart understanding, making it a strong foundation for vision encoders in MLLMs.

</details>


### [47] [Posterior Augmented Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00825)
*George Stoica,Sayak Paul,Matthew Wallingford,Vivek Ramanujan,Abhay Nori,Winson Han,Ali Farhadi,Ranjay Krishna,Judy Hoffman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出后验增强流匹配（PAFM），通过用近似后验的期望替代单目标监督，显著降低流匹配训练中的梯度方差，在多种模型规模和架构上提升FID达3.4，而计算开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配（FM）在高维图像训练中，每个样本仅监督单一轨迹和中间点，导致训练信号极其稀疏且方差大，这种欠约束监督可能引发流坍缩——模型记忆特定源-目标配对，将多样输入映射到过度相似的输出，泛化能力差。

Method: PAFM将不可解的后验分解为(i)中间状态在假设终点下的似然与(ii)该终点在条件下的先验概率的乘积，并采用重要性采样构建多个候选目标的混合分布，用该近似后验的期望替代单目标监督。

Result: PAFM是原始FM目标的无偏估计量，同时显著降低梯度方差。在SiT-B/2和SiT-XL/2规模、SiT和MMDiT架构、ImageNet和CC12M数据集上，PAFM相比FM提升FID50K最多达3.4，计算开销可忽略。

Conclusion: PAFM从理论上证明了其估计量的无偏性，并通过聚合每个中间状态的多条合理连续轨迹信息，有效缓解了高维图像生成中流匹配训练信号稀疏和方差大的问题，显著提升生成质量。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) trains a time-dependent vector field that transports samples from a simple prior to a complex data distribution. However, for high-dimensional images, each training sample supervises only a single trajectory and intermediate point, yielding an extremely sparse and high-variance training signal. This under-constrained supervision can cause flow collapse, where the learned dynamics memorize specific source-target pairings, mapping diverse inputs to overly similar outputs, failing to generalize. We introduce Posterior-Augmented Flow Matching (PAFM), a theoretically grounded generalization of FM that replaces single-target supervision with an expectation over an approximate posterior of valid target completions for a given intermediate state and condition. PAFM factorizes this intractable posterior into (i) the likelihood of the intermediate under a hypothesized endpoint and (ii) the prior probability of that endpoint under the condition, and uses an importance sampling scheme to construct a mixture over multiple candidate targets. We prove that PAFM yields an unbiased estimator of the original FM objective while substantially reducing gradient variance during training by aggregating information from many plausible continuation trajectories per intermediate. Finally, we show that PAFM improves over FM by up to 3.4 FID50K across different model scales (SiT-B/2 and SiT-XL/2), different architectures (SiT and MMDiT), and in both class and text conditioned benchmarks (ImageNet and CC12M), with a negligible increase in the compute overhead. Code: https://github.com/gstoica27/PAFM.git.

</details>

### [48] [Dynamic-TD3: A Novel Algorithm for UAV Path Planning with Dynamic Obstacle Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00059)
*Wentao Chen,Jingtang Chen,Mingjian Fu,Tiantian Li,Youfeng Su,Wenxi Liu,Yuanlong Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Dynamic-TD3框架，通过物理增强的约束马尔可夫决策过程解决无人机导航中的安全-探索困境，集成ATREM和PAG-KF实现严格安全约束下的高效机动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在无人机自主导航中面临安全-探索困境：软惩罚机制鼓励危险试错，而现有约束方法在传感器噪声和意图不确定性下性能退化，亟需一种既能保证严格安全约束又能维持机动性的方法。

Method: 将导航建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程(CMDP)，集成自适应轨迹关系演化机制(ATREM)捕获长程意图，采用物理感知门控卡尔曼滤波(PAG-KF)抑制非平稳观测噪声，通过拉格朗日松弛构建双准则策略平衡任务效率与硬安全约束。

Result: 在激进动态威胁场景下，该方法展现出优越的碰撞 avoidance 性能、更低的能耗和更平滑的飞行轨迹。

Conclusion: Dynamic-TD3框架有效解决了DRN在复杂环境中的安全-探索困境，实现了严格安全约束与高机动性的统一，为无人机的实际部署提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) finds extensive application in autonomous drone navigation within complex, high-risk environments. However, its practical deployment faces a safety-exploration dilemma: soft penalty mechanisms encourage risky trial-and-error, while most constraint-based methods suffer degraded performance under sensor noise and intent uncertainty. We propose Dynamic-TD3, a physically enhanced framework that enforces strict safety constraints while maintaining maneuverability by modeling navigation as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP). This framework integrates an Adaptive Trajectory Relational Evolution Mechanism (ATREM) to capture long-range intentions and employs a Physically Aware Gated Kalman Filter (PAG-KF) to mitigate non-stationary observation noise. The resulting state representation drives a dual-criterion policy that balances mission efficiency against hard safety constraints via Lagrangian relaxation. In experiments with aggressive dynamic threats, this approach demonstrates superior collision avoidance performance, reduced energy consumption, and smoother flight trajectories.

</details>


### [49] [Do Open-Loop Metrics Predict Closed-Loop Driving? A Cross-Benchmark Correlation Study of NAVSIM and Bench2Drive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00066)
*Yiru Wang,Anqing Jiang,Shuo Wang,Yuwen Heng,Hai Yang,Yang Chen,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了NAVSIM v2（开环）与Bench2Drive（闭环）中15种方法的性能，发现：聚合PDM分数与闭环驾驶分数呈强正相关但非单调；Ego Progress是闭环成功的最佳单一预测因子；安全与进展的权衡在开环和闭环中表现不同。提出了一个简化的3指标公式，在n=8个方法上达到了与完整5指标PDMS相同的预测能力（Spearman ρ=0.90）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开环评估能否预测闭环驾驶性能一直存在争议，传统开环指标（ADE/FDE）与闭环驾驶分数无可靠相关性。本文旨在检验NAVSIM v2引入的更具安全意识的开环指标是否能弥合这一差距。

Method: 系统交叉引用NAVSIM（开环）和Bench2Drive（闭环）中15种方法的公开结果，构建开环子指标与闭环性能的配对数据集（最终8种方法有完整配对数据），进行相关性分析和指标比较。

Result: (1) PDM分数与闭环驾驶分数强正相关但非单调，存在排名反转；(2) Ego Progress是闭环成功的最佳单一预测因子，远超碰撞指标NC；(3) 安全-进展权衡在开环与闭环中表现不同；简化的3指标公式达到与完整5指标PDMS相同的预测能力（ρ=0.90）；识别了"snowball effect"作为残余差距的候选机制。

Conclusion: 当前SOTA方法中，TTC和Comfort指标接近饱和，对闭环排名贡献有限；开环评估向闭环迁移仍需谨慎，尤其是安全-进展权衡的差异需被纳入考量。

Abstract: Open-loop evaluation offers fast, reproducible assessment of autonomous driving planners, but its ability to predict real closed-loop driving performance remains questionable. Prior work has shown that traditional open-loop metrics such as Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) exhibit no reliable correlation with closed-loop Driving Score. In this paper, we ask whether the more recent, safety-aware open-loop metrics introduced by NAVSIM~v2 can bridge this gap. By systematically cross-referencing published results from 15 state-of-the-art methods across NAVSIM (open-loop) and Bench2Drive (closed-loop), we compile a paired dataset of open-loop sub-metrics and closed-loop performance, yielding 8 methods with complete paired data. Our analysis reveals three key findings: (1) the aggregate NAVSIM PDM Score shows a strong positive but non-monotonic correlation with Bench2Drive Driving Score, with clear ranking inversions; (2) among individual NAVSIM sub-metrics, Ego Progress (EP) is the strongest single predictor of closed-loop success, substantially exceeding the safety-critical collision metric NC; (3) the safety-progress trade-off manifests differently in open-loop and closed-loop: methods that maximize safety at the expense of progress rank highly in NAVSIM but underperform in closed-loop due to timeout and slow-driving penalties. We further demonstrate that a much simpler 3-metric formula matches the predictive power of the full 5-metric PDMS at the same Spearman $ρ{=}0.90$ on our paired sample of $n{=}8$ methods, suggesting that within current state-of-the-art methods -- where TTC and Comfort approach saturation -- these two sub-metrics add little marginal information for closed-loop ranking. Additionally, we identify the snowball effect -- where small open-loop deviations compound into closed-loop failures -- as a candidate mechanism for the residual gap.

</details>


### [50] [Being-H0.7: A Latent World-Action Model from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00078)
*Hao Luo,Wanpeng Zhang,Yicheng Feng,Sipeng Zheng,Haiweng Xu,Chaoyi Xu,Ziheng Xi,Yuhui Fu,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Being-H0.7是一种潜空间世界-动作模型，通过可学习的潜查询和未来感知双分支设计，在不生成未来帧的情况下为VLA策略注入未来推理能力，在多个仿真和真实世界任务中达到SOTA或相当性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型存在稀疏动作监督导致的捷径映射问题，难以学习动力学、接触和任务进展等结构化表示；而基于像素预测的世界模型训练/推理开销大，且建模了大量与控制无关的视觉细节。

Method: 在感知和动作之间插入可学习的潜查询作为紧凑推理接口，采用未来感知双分支设计：可部署的先验分支从当前上下文推断潜状态，仅训练的后验分支用未来观测嵌入替换查询，在潜推理空间对齐两个分支，使先验分支学会从当前观测推理未来感知、动作有用的结构。推理时丢弃后验分支，无视觉 rollout。

Result: 在六个仿真基准和多种真实世界任务中达到SOTA或相当性能，兼具世界模型的预测优势与直接VLA策略的高效可部署性。

Conclusion: 潜空间未来预测是一种比像素空间预测更高效且有效的控制推理方式，能够在不增加推理开销的情况下提升VLA策略的任务表现和结构化理解能力。

Abstract: Visual-Language-Action models (VLAs) have advanced generalist robot control by mapping multimodal observations and language instructions directly to actions, but sparse action supervision often encourages shortcut mappings rather than representations of dynamics, contact, and task progress. Recent world-action models introduce future prediction through video rollouts, yet pixel-space prediction is a costly and indirect substrate for control, as it may model visual details irrelevant to action generation and introduces substantial training or inference overhead. We present Being-H0.7, a latent world-action model that brings future-aware reasoning into VLA-style policies without generating future frames. Being-H0.7 inserts learnable latent queries between perception and action as a compact reasoning interface, and trains them with a future-informed dual-branch design: a deployable prior branch infers latent states from the current context, while a training-only posterior branch replaces the queries with embeddings from future observations. Jointly aligning the two branches at the latent reasoning space leads the prior branch to reason future-aware, action-useful structure from current observations alone. At inference, Being-H0.7 discards the posterior branch and performs no visual rollout. Experiments across six simulation benchmarks and diverse real-world tasks show that Being-H0.7 achieves state-of-the-art or comparable performance, combining the predictive benefits of world models with the efficiency and deployability of direct VLA policies.

</details>


### [51] [World Model for Robot Learning: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00080)
*Bohan Hou,Gen Li,Jindou Jia,Tuo An,Xinying Guo,Sicong Leng,Haoran Geng,Yanjie Ze,Tatsuya Harada,Philip Torr,Oier Mees,Marc Pollefeys,Zhuang Liu,Jiajun Wu,Pieter Abbeel,Jitendra Malik,Yilun Du,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了机器人学习视角下的世界模型研究，涵盖其与策略的耦合方式、作为强化学习模拟器的角色、视频世界模型的发展历程，以及在导航和自动驾驶中的应用，并总结了相关数据集、基准和评估协议，最后指出了主要挑战和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中世界模型的相关文献在架构、功能角色和具身应用领域方面存在碎片化，缺乏系统性的梳理和整合，亟需一份全面的综述来澄清关键范式和应用。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，从机器人学习视角出发，考察世界模型与机器人策略的耦合方式、作为强化学习模拟器的功能、视频世界模型从想象生成到可控结构化及基础模型规模的发展，以及导航和自动驾驶中的应用，同时总结代表性数据集、基准和评估协议。

Result: 梳理了世界模型在机器人学习中的关键范式和应用，包括策略耦合、模拟器角色、视频生成技术的发展脉络，以及在导航和自动驾驶中的具体应用，并汇总了相关数据和评估资源。

Conclusion: 该综述为快速增长的机器人学习世界模型领域提供了系统性梳理，明确了主要挑战和未来研究方向，并将通过持续更新的GitHub仓库保持对新工作的跟踪。

Abstract: World models, which are predictive representations of how environments evolve under actions, have become a central component of robot learning. They support policy learning, planning, simulation, evaluation, data generation, and have advanced rapidly with the rise of foundation models and large-scale video generation. However, the literature remains fragmented across architectures, functional roles, and embodied application domains. To address this gap, we present a comprehensive review of world models from a robot-learning perspective. We examine how world models are coupled with robot policies, how they serve as learned simulators for reinforcement learning and evaluation, and how robotic video world models have progressed from imagination-based generation to controllable, structured, and foundation-scale formulations. We further connect these ideas to navigation and autonomous driving, and summarize representative datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation protocols. Overall, this survey systematically reviews the rapidly growing literature on world models for robot learning, clarifies key paradigms and applications, and highlights major challenges and future directions for predictive modeling in embodied agents. To facilitate continued access to newly emerging works, benchmarks, and resources, we will maintain and regularly update the accompanying GitHub repository alongside this survey.

</details>


### [52] [Predictive Spatio-Temporal Scene Graphs for Semi-Static Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00121)
*Miguel Saavedra-Ruiz,Charlie Gauthier,Kumaraditya Gupta,Shima Shahfar,Kirsty Ellis,Steven Parkison,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PredictiveGraphs方法，通过将贝叶斯滤波器Perpetua$^*$集成到3D场景图中，实现了对半静态环境中物体状态变化的时序推理与预测，在模拟和真实环境（三周、每两小时变化）中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的"时空-语义"表征方法大多缺乏跨时间推理能力，无法捕捉环境中物体在多次观测之间发生的结构化变化模式（如杯子在橱柜、台面、水槽之间的循环移动），因此需要一种能够进行时间-空间-语义联合推理的方法。

Method: 提出PredictiveGraphs结构，其中节点表示物体，边通过Perpetua$^*$滤波器编码时空-语义关系；Perpetua$^*$是一种贝叶斯滤波器，对随时间观测到的环境状态进行贝叶斯推理，实现对未来状态的预测。

Result: 在模拟环境和真实世界动态导航任务中验证，真实实验历时三周、每两小时记录一次半静态变化；所提方法在预测未来环境状态方面优于基线，即使在分布偏移情况下仍保持优势。

Conclusion: 将贝叶斯时序滤波与3D场景图结构相结合，能够有效学习环境中物体状态的结构化变化模式并进行准确预测，为机器人长期自主运行中的时空推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We have seen tremendous recent progress in our ability to build "spatio-semantic" representations that enable robots to perform complex reasoning across geometry and semantics. However, the vast majority of these methods lack any ability to perform reasoning across time. This is a desirable property in situations where a robot repeatedly observes an environment where instances may change in between observations, but in a structured way. Consider as an example a home environment where the location of a mug typically moves from the cupboard to a countertop to the sink and then back to the cupboard on a daily basis. We should be able to learn this cyclic behavior and use it to predict the state of the mug in the future. In this work, we propose a method that is able to perform this type of tempo-spatio-semantic reasoning. Underpinning the method is a filter, Perpetua$^*$, that performs Bayesian reasoning on the states of the environment that are observed over time. This filter is integrated within a 3D scene graph structure that we call PredictiveGraphs, where nodes represent objects and edges function as Perpetua$^*$ filters encoding spatio-semantic relationships. We validate the method in both simulation and real-world dynamic navigation tasks, where our real world experiments consist of an environment that is undergoing semi-static changes at a bi-hourly frequency over a period of three weeks. In both settings, we demonstrate that our method outperforms baselines in predicting future environment states, even in the presence of distributional shifts.

</details>


### [53] [E$^2$DT: Efficient and Effective Decision Transformer with Experience-Aware Sampling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00159)
*Kaiyan Zhao,Borong Zhang,Yiming Wang,Xingyu Liu,Xuetao Li,Yuyang Chen,Xiaoguang Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出E²DT框架，通过基于k-DPP的体验采样方法，使Decision Transformer能够主动选择高质量、多样化的经验轨迹，在机器人操作任务中实现高效且有效的长时序学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DT依赖于均匀回放，缺乏主动探索机制，导致样本效率低、探索受限；而过度探索虽能避免局部最优，但会延迟策略收敛、降低效率。需要一种既能保证采样质量（高回报、高不确定性、低表示轨迹）又能保证采样多样性（跨轨迹窗口）的体验选择机制。

Method: 提出E²DT框架，核心是DT引导的k-Determinantal Point Process（k-DPP）采样框架。利用DT内部潜在嵌入衡量跨轨迹窗口的多样性，通过结合RTG分位数、预测不确定性和基于逆频率的阶段覆盖度构建复合质量指标，并将多样性和质量集成到质量-多样性联合核中，优先选择最具信息量的经验。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作基准上，E²DT持续优于先前方法，证明了将策略学习与体验感知采样相结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 耦合策略学习与体验感知采样为鲁棒的长时序机器人学习提供了一条原则性路径。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic manipulation, the Decision Transformer (DT) has emerged as an effective framework for addressing long-horizon tasks. However, DT's performance depends heavily on the coverage of collected experiences. Without an active exploration mechanism, standard DT relies on uniform replay, which leads to poor sample efficiency, limited exploration, and reduced overall effectiveness. At the same time, while excessive exploration can help avoid local optima, it often delays policy convergence and leads to degraded efficiency. To address these limitations, we propose E$^2$DT, a DT-guided k-Determinantal Point Process sampling framework that enables the model to actively shape its own experience selection. Our framework is experience-aware, allowing E$^2$DT to be both efficient, by prioritizing sampling quality, such as high-return, high-uncertainty, and underrepresented trajectories, and effective, by ensuring diversity across trajectory windows to preserve policy optimality. Specifically, DT's internal latent embeddings measure diversity across trajectory windows, while quality is quantified through a composite metric that integrates return-to-go (RTG) quantiles, predictive uncertainty, and stage coverage based on inverse frequency. These two dimensions are integrated into a novel quality-diversity joint kernel that prioritizes the most informative experiences, thereby enabling learning that is both efficient and effective. We evaluate E$^2$DT on challenging robotic manipulation benchmarks in both simulation and real-robot settings. Results show that it consistently outperforms prior methods. These findings demonstrate that coupling policy learning with experience-aware sampling provides a principled path toward robust long-horizon robotic learning.

</details>


### [54] [Lucid-XR: An Extended-Reality Data Engine for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00244)
*Yajvan Ravan,Adam Rashid,Alan Yu,Kai McClennen,Gio Huh,Kevin Yang,Zhutian Yang,Qinxi Yu,Xiaolong Wang,Phillip Isola,Ge Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Lucid-XR是一个生成式数据引擎，通过在XR头显上运行基于web的物理模拟环境vuer，结合人体到机器人的姿态重定向和物理引导的视频生成管道，为真实世界机器人系统生成多样化、逼真的多模态训练数据，实现零样本策略迁移到未见过的复杂环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人训练数据获取成本高、多样性不足，且依赖特殊设备。Lucid-XR旨在通过无需专业设备的可扩展XR模拟环境，生成大规模、多样化的多模态数据，以训练能零样本迁移到真实复杂环境的机器人策略。

Method: 1) vuer：基于web的物理模拟环境，直接在XR头显上运行，提供低延迟沉浸式交互；2) 在设备端集成物理模拟与人体到机器人的姿态重定向；3) 物理引导的视频生成管道，可通过自然语言控制数据增强。

Result: 在仅使用Lucid-XR合成数据训练后，机器人视觉策略实现了对未见过的杂乱、光照差评估环境的零样本迁移，验证了在涉及软材料、松散颗粒和刚体接触的灵巧操作任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: Lucid-XR展示了通过可扩展的XR模拟引擎和生成式数据增强，无需真实数据即可训练出能泛化到极具挑战的真实环境的机器人策略，为机器人数据生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce Lucid-XR, a generative data engine for creating diverse and realistic-looking multi-modal data to train real-world robotic systems. At the core of Lucid-XR is vuer, a web-based physics simulation environment that runs directly on the XR headset, enabling internet-scale access to immersive, latency-free virtual interactions without requiring specialized equipment. The complete system integrates on-device physics simulation with human-to-robot pose retargeting. Data collected is further amplified by a physics-guided video generation pipeline steerable via natural language specifications. We demonstrate zero-shot transfer of robot visual policies to unseen, cluttered, and badly lit evaluation environments, after training entirely on Lucid-XR's synthetic data. We include examples across dexterous manipulation tasks that involve soft materials, loosely bound particles, and rigid body contact. Project website: https://lucidxr.github.io

</details>


### [55] [Task-Conditioned Uncertainty Costmaps for Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00261)
*Kartikeya Singh,Christo Aluckal,Romeo Orsolino,Karthik Dantu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过建模足式机器人足迹预测中的认知不确定性，来区分分布内和分布外（OOD）地形，并将不确定性纳入成本地图生成框架，实现不确定性感知的路径规划。实验表明该方法在仿真和真实场景中将OOD检测的可行性误差降低高达37%，优于仅依赖几何信息的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人在高度非结构化地形上进行多接触交互时，准确预测未来足迹非常困难，即使采用重复步态周期。现有模型在训练数据覆盖不足的OOD场景中可靠性差，需要一种能够表达模型不确定性的方法。

Method: 在足迹预测中建模认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty），以地形观测和指令运动为条件，区分ID和OOD操作场景。将学习到的不确定性用于检测OOD区域，并融入统一的成本地图生成框架，实现不确定性感知的路径规划。

Result: 仿真和真实场景中，OOD检测得到改善，可行性误差降低高达37%，相比仅使用几何信息的基线方法，规划行为更可靠。

Conclusion: 单个训练于有限数据分布上的学习模型，通过表达由训练覆盖缺失引起的不确定性，能够有效处理OOD场景，并为不确定性感知的运动规划提供实用框架。

Abstract: Legged robots maintain dynamic feasibility through multicontact interactions with terrain. Learned foothold prediction can provide feasibility-aware costs for motion planning and path selection, but accurately predicting future contacts from perceptual inputs such as height scans remains challenging on highly unstructured terrain, even with a repetitive gait cycle. In this work, we show that modeling epistemic uncertainty in predicted footholds, conditioned on terrain observations and commanded motion, distinguishes in-distribution from out-of-distribution operating regimes in simulation and real-world settings. This allows a single learned model, trained on limited data distributions, to express uncertainty caused by missing training coverage. We use this learned uncertainty to detect OOD regions and incorporate them into a unified costmap-generation framework for uncertainty-aware path planning. Using these uncertainty-aware costmaps, we evaluate feasibility error across in-distribution and OOD terrains in simulation and real-world settings. The results show improved OOD detection, up to a 37% reduction in simulation feasibility error, and more reliable planning behavior than geometry-only baselines.

</details>


### [56] [A Model-based Visual Contact Localization and Force Sensing System for Compliant Robotic Grippers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00307)
*Kaiwen Zuo,Shuyuan Yang,Zonghe Chua*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型的视觉力传感方法，利用腕部RGB-D相机对变形的鳍条状软体夹爪进行结构关键点提取，结合逆有限元分析仿真和迭代接触定位（基于深度学习3D重建与位姿估计），实现了对未知物体的抓取力估计，负载阶段RMSE为0.23 N，整体抓取过程为0.48 N。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取力估计方法中，端到端深度学习泛化性差，而基于模型的方法不适用于软体夹爪的几何形状和抓取场景。为了在软体夹爪中实现低成本、高鲁棒性、且能泛化到未知物体的间接视觉力感知，亟需一种结合模型驱动与视觉信息的解决方案。

Method: 从腕部RGB-D相机中提取软体鳍条状夹爪变形的结构关键点，利用这些关键点定义逆有限元分析（FEA）仿真的参数（在SOFA框架中运行）；同时构建迭代接触定位子系统，基于深度学习的在线3D重建与位姿估计动态更新接触位置，对视觉遮挡和未知物体具有鲁棒性。

Result: 负载阶段平均RMSE为0.23 N，归一化均方根偏差（NRMSD）为2.11%；整个抓取过程平均RMSE为0.48 N，NRMSD为4.34%。在不同物体和各种条件下均表现出良好的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了基于模型的实时间接视觉力传感在软体夹爪中的潜力，兼具模型驱动的泛化能力和深度学习的适应性，为机器人精细操作提供了一种有效的力估计方案。

Abstract: Grasp force estimation can help prevent robots from damaging delicate objects during manipulation and improve learning-based robotic control. Integrating force sensing into deformable grippers negotiates trade-offs in cost, complexity, mechanical robustness, and performance. With the growing integration of RGB-D wrist cameras into robotic systems for control purposes, camera-based techniques are a promising solution for indirect visual force estimation. Current approaches mostly utilize end-to-end deep learning, which can be brittle when generalizing to new scenarios, while existing model-based approaches are unsuited to grasping and modern grasper geometries. To address these challenges, we developed a model-based visual force sensing approach integrating an iterative contact localization with generalization to unseen objects. The system extracts structural key points from wrist camera RGB-D images of deforming fin-ray-shaped soft grippers, and uses these key points to define parameters of an inverse finite element analysis simulation in Simulation Open Framework Architecture. The iterative contact localization sub-system utilizes a deep learning-based online 3D reconstruction and pose estimation pipeline to dynamically update contact location, and is robust to visual occlusion and unseen objects. Our system demonstrated an average root mean square error of 0.23 N and normalized root mean square deviation of 2.11% during the load phase, and 0.48 N and 4.34% over the entire grasping process when interacting with different objects under various conditions, showcasing its potential for real-time model-based indirect force sensing of soft grippers.

</details>


### [57] [PrefMoE: Robust Preference Modeling with Mixture-of-Experts Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00384)
*Ziqin Yuan,Ruiqi Wang,Dezhong Zhao,Baijian Yang,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PrefMoE提出了一种基于混合专家（MoE）的奖励学习框架，通过多个奖励专家和轨迹级软路由来应对偏好数据中的异质性和噪声，从而提升鲁棒性和下游策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于偏好的强化学习方法在面对大规模偏好数据中的标注者间分歧和标注者内部不一致时，只能通过单一奖励模型平均这些矛盾信号，导致鲁棒性受限。

Method: 提出PrefMoE框架，学习多个专门化的奖励专家，通过轨迹级软路由自适应组合这些专家，并引入负载均衡正则化器防止专家崩溃，以捕捉多样化潜在偏好模式。

Result: 在D4RL的 locomotion 基准和MetaWorld的操作任务上，PrefMoE在偏好预测鲁棒性和下游策略学习可靠性方面均优于强单模型基线。

Conclusion: PrefMoE通过混合专家架构有效解决了异构和噪声偏好监督下的奖励学习鲁棒性问题。

Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning offers a scalable alternative to manual reward engineering by learning reward structures from comparative feedback. However, large-scale preference datasets, whether collected from crowdsourced annotators or generated by synthetic teachers, often contain heterogeneous and partially conflicting supervision, including disagreement across annotators and inconsistency within annotators. Existing reward learning methods typically fit a single reward model to such data, forcing it to average incompatible signals and thereby limiting robustness. To solve this, we propose PrefMoE, a mixture-of-experts reward learning framework for robust preference modeling. PrefMoE learns multiple specialized reward experts and uses trajectory-level soft routing to combine them adaptively, enabling the model to capture diverse latent preference patterns under noisy and heterogeneous preference supervision. A load-balancing regularizer further stabilizes training by preventing expert collapse. Across locomotion benchmarks from D4RL and manipulation tasks from MetaWorld, PrefMoE improves preference prediction robustness and leads to more reliable downstream policy learning than strong single-model baselines.

</details>


### [58] [MiniVLA-Nav v1: A Multi-Scene Simulation Dataset for Language-Conditioned Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00397)
*Ali Al-Bustami,Jaerock Kwon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiniVLA-Nav v1 是一个面向语言条件物体接近导航任务的仿真数据集，包含1,174个episode，在四种Isaac Sim环境中提供RGB图像、深度图、实例分割掩码及专家动作标签，支持多维度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补语言条件导航领域中仿真数据集的空白，为物体接近导航任务提供标准化、多样化且带有精确标签的训练与评估资源。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Nova Carter差分驱动机器人，在四个Isaac Sim环境中采集数据；通过视觉比例控制器生成连续(v,omega)和7x7 tokenized专家动作；设置三类生成距离层级、12种物体类别、18个训练模板和12个释义OOD模板确保轨迹多样性。

Result: 数据集包含1,174个episode，640x640 RGB图像、float32深度图、实例分割掩码及60Hz专家动作标签；生成距离与轨迹长度相关性达Pearson r=0.94；提供五种评估切分支持分布内准确率、模板释义鲁棒性和OOD物体类别基准测试。

Conclusion: MiniVLA-Nav v1为语言条件导航研究提供了一个高质量、多样化且公开可用的仿真数据集，可支撑从分布内评估到OOD泛化的多维度实验。

Abstract: We present MiniVLA-Nav v1, a simulation dataset for Language-Conditioned Object Approach (LCOA) navigation: given a short natural-language instruction, an NVIDIA Nova Carter differential-drive robot must navigate to the named object and stop within 1 m across four photorealistic Isaac Sim environments (Office, Hospital, Full Warehouse, and Warehouse with Multiple Shelves). Each of the 1,174 episodes pairs an instruction with synchronized 640x640 RGB images, metric depth maps (float32, metres), and instance segmentation masks, together with continuous (v,omega) and 7x7 tokenized expert action labels recorded at 60 Hz from a vision-based proportional controller. Trajectory diversity is ensured through three spawn-distance tiers (near: 1.5-3.5 m, mid: 3.5-7.0 m, far: global curated points; Pearson r=0.94 between spawn distance and trajectory length), 12 object categories, 18 training templates, and 12 paraphrase-OOD templates. Five evaluation splits support in-distribution accuracy, template-paraphrase robustness, and OOD object-category benchmarking. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/alibustami/miniVLA-Nav

</details>


### [59] [Learning while Deploying: Fleet-Scale Reinforcement Learning for Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00416)
*Yi Wang,Xinchen Li,Pengwei Xie,Pu Yang,Buqing Nie,Yunuo Cai,Qinglin Zhang,Chendi Qu,Jeffrey Wu,Jianheng Song,Xinlin Ren,Jingshun Huang,Mingjie Pan,Siyuan Feng,Zhi Chen,Jianlan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LWD框架，实现通用VLA机器人在部署阶段的持续在线强化学习后训练，通过车队级数据闭环提升性能，最终在16台双臂机器人上达到95%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据不足以应对部署中的分布偏移、长尾故障、任务变化等挑战，需要利用部署阶段的自主交互和人类干预数据进行持续的在线后训练。

Method: 提出LWD框架，包含：1) 部署-经验收集-策略改进-重新部署的闭环流程；2) Distributional Implicit Value Learning (DIVL) 用于鲁棒的价值估计；3) Q-learning via Adjoint Matching (QAM) 用于基于流的VLA动作生成器的策略提取。

Result: 在16台双臂机器人的8项真实操作任务中，单一的通用策略持续提升，平均成功率95%，长时域任务提升最为显著。

Conclusion: LWD证明了从离线预训练VLA策略出发，通过车队级在线强化学习可以实现持续、稳定的策略改进，有效克服了异构稀疏奖励数据的学习挑战。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies increasingly benefit from large-scale pretraining, but offline data alone is insufficient for robust real-world deployment. Deployed robots encounter distribution shifts, long-tail failures, task variations, and human correction opportunities that fixed demonstration datasets cannot fully capture. We present Learning While Deploying (LWD), a fleet-scale offline-to-online reinforcement learning framework for continual post-training of generalist Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies. Starting from a pretrained VLA policy, LWD closes the loop between deployment, shared physical experience, policy improvement, and redeployment by using autonomous rollouts and human interventions collected across a robot fleet. To stabilize learning from heterogeneous, sparse-reward fleet data, LWD combines Distributional Implicit Value Learning (DIVL) for robust value estimation with Q-learning via Adjoint Matching (QAM) for policy extraction in flow-based VLA action generators. We validate LWD on a fleet of 16 dual-arm robots across eight real-world manipulation tasks, including semantic grocery restocking and 3--5 minute long-horizon tasks. A single generalist policy improves as fleet experience accumulates, reaching an average success rate of 95%, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [60] [Stereo Multistage Spatial Attention for Real-Time Mobile Manipulation Under Visual Scale Variation and Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00471)
*Xianbo Cai,Hideyuki Ichiwara,Hyogo Hiruma,Masaki Yoshikawa,Hiroshi Ito,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于立体多级空间注意力深度预测学习的移动操作实时方法，通过从立体图像中提取任务相关空间注意力点，结合层级循环架构进行闭环动作预测，在四项真实世界移动操作任务中，相比模仿学习和视觉-语言-动作基线方法，在鲁棒性和任务成功率上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在开放非结构化环境中依赖机载视觉感知自主移动时，车载摄像机视点的持续变化会导致目标物体视觉尺度显著变化，从而影响基于视觉的运动生成。现有方法在应对此类视觉变化时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出立体多级空间注意力深度预测学习方法；从立体图像中提取任务相关的空间注意力点；通过层级循环架构将空间注意力点与机器人状态融合，实现闭环动作预测。

Result: 在真实移动操作平台上评估四项任务（刚性放置、铰接物体操作、可变形物体交互），在随机初始位置和视觉干扰条件下，相比代表性模仿学习和视觉-语言-动作基线方法，任务成功率和鲁棒性均有提升。

Conclusion: 结构化的立体空间注意力结合预测性时序建模，在所评估的移动操作场景中提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Robots operating in open, unstructured real-world environments must rely on onboard visual perception while autonomously moving across different locations. Continuous changes in onboard camera viewpoints cause significant visual scale variations in target objects, affecting vision-based motion generation. In this work, we present a stereo multistage spatial attention-based deep predictive learning method for real-time mobile manipulation. The proposed methods extracts task-relevant spatial attention points from stereo images and integrates them with robot states through a hierarchical recurrent architecture for closed-loop action prediction. We evaluate the system on four real-world mobile manipulation tasks using a mobile manipulator, including rigid placement, articulated object manipulation, and deformable object interaction. Experiments under randomized initial positions and visual disturbance conditions demonstrate improved robustness and task success rates compared to representative imitation learning and vision-language-action baselines under identical control settings. The results indicate that structured stereo spatial attention combined with predictive temporal modeling provides an effective solution within the evaluated mobile manipulation scenarios.

</details>


### [61] [MSACT: Multistage Spatial Alignment for Stable Low-Latency Fine Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00475)
*Xianbo Cai,Hideyuki Ichiwara,Masaki Yoshikawa,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多阶段空间注意力模块，基于ACT架构，通过自监督时间对齐损失预测未来注意力序列，在有限数据下实现低延迟、高稳定的双臂精细操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实双臂精细操作需要低延迟控制和稳定的视觉定位，但大规模数据收集成本高，有限演示会导致定位漂移。现有方法在延迟、数据效率、空间一致性之间难以兼顾。

Method: 在ACT基础上引入多阶段空间注意力模块，提取稳定的2D注意力点，联合预测未来注意力序列并施加时间对齐自监督损失，利用预训练ResNet视觉先验，无需关键点标注即可抑制漂移。

Result: 在ALOHA双臂平台的仿真和真实精细操作任务中，该方法在任务成功率、注意力漂移抑制、推理延迟和视觉扰动鲁棒性方面均有提升，同时保持低延迟推理。

Conclusion: 所提方法在有限数据下有效提升了视觉到动作映射的稳定性和任务性能，兼顾了低延迟与定位鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-world fine manipulation, particularly in bimanual manipulation, typically requires low-latency control and stable visual localization, while collecting large-scale data is costly and limited demonstrations may lead to localization drift. Existing approaches make different trade-offs: action-chunking policies such as ACT enable low-latency execution and data efficiency but rely on dense visual features without explicit spatial consistency, generative methods such as Diffusion Policy improve expressiveness but can incur iterative sampling latency, vision-language-action and voxel-based methods enhance generalization and geometric grounding but require higher computational cost and system complexity. We introduce a multistage spatial attention module that extracts stable 2D attention points and jointly predicts future attention sequences with a temporal alignment loss. Built upon ACT with a pretrained ResNet visual prior, a multistage attention module extracts task-relevant 2D attention points as a local spatial modality for action prediction. To maintain consistent object tracking, we introduce a self-supervised objective that aligns predicted attention sequences with visual features from future frames, suppressing drift without keypoint annotations and improving stability of the vision-to-action mapping under limited data. Experiments on simulated and real-world fine manipulation tasks, conducted on the ALOHA bimanual platform, evaluate task success, attention drift, inference latency, and robustness to visual disturbances. Results indicate improvements in localization stability and task performance while maintaining low-latency inference under the tested conditions.

</details>


### [62] [Recovering Hidden Reward in Diffusion-Based Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00623)
*Yanbiao Ji,Qiuchang Li,Yuting Hu,Shaokai Wu,Wenyuan Xie,Guodong Zhang,Qicheng He,Deyi Ji,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EnergyFlow提出了一种统一生成式动作建模与逆强化学习的框架，通过参数化标量能量函数（其梯度为去噪场），在最大熵最优性下证明了去噪评分匹配学习的评分函数能够恢复专家软Q函数的梯度，从而无需对抗训练即可提取奖励函数。理论上证明了保守场约束降低了假设复杂度并收紧了分布外泛化边界，经验上在多种操作任务中达到了最先进的模仿性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逆强化学习方法通常依赖对抗训练，存在训练不稳定、收敛困难等问题；而生成式动作建模虽然效果好，但缺乏明确的奖励函数提取机制。作者希望统一这两个范式，在保持生成式建模优势的同时，实现无需对抗训练的奖励提取。

Method: 参数化一个标量能量函数，其梯度定义为去噪场；通过去噪评分匹配学习评分函数，在最大熵最优性假设下证明其恢复专家软Q函数的梯度；对学习场施加保守约束（conservative constraint）以降低假设复杂度并收紧OOD泛化界；理论分析了奖励的可识别性以及评分估计误差对动作偏好的传播。

Result: 在多种操作任务上实现了最先进的模仿性能；提取的奖励信号在下游强化学习中优于对抗性IRL方法和基于似然的方法；验证了用于有效奖励提取的结构约束同时作为有利于策略泛化的归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 能量函数的结构约束（保守性）不仅保证了奖励提取的有效性，还作为有益的归纳偏置提升了策略的泛化能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper introduces EnergyFlow, a framework that unifies generative action modeling with inverse reinforcement learning by parameterizing a scalar energy function whose gradient is the denoising field. We establish that under maximum-entropy optimality, the score function learned via denoising score matching recovers the gradient of the expert's soft Q-function, enabling reward extraction without adversarial training. Formally, we prove that constraining the learned field to be conservative reduces hypothesis complexity and tightens out-of-distribution generalization bounds. We further characterize the identifiability of recovered rewards and bound how score estimation errors propagate to action preferences. Empirically, EnergyFlow achieves state-of-the-art imitation performance on various manipulation tasks while providing an effective reward signal for downstream reinforcement learning that outperforms both adversarial IRL methods and likelihood-based alternatives. These results show that the structural constraints required for valid reward extraction simultaneously serve as beneficial inductive biases for policy generalization. The code is available at https://github.com/sotaagi/EnergyFlow.

</details>


### [63] [Paired-CSLiDAR: Height-Stratified Registration for Cross-Source Aerial-Ground LiDAR Pose Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00634)
*Montana Hoover,Jing Liang,Tianrui Guan,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Paired-CSLiDAR跨源空地LiDAR基准和RGSR配准方法，用于单扫描位姿精化，在50米半径内实现86.0%的S@0.75m精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地LiDAR扫描几何模态差异大（航空捕捉屋顶树冠，地面捕捉立面冠下），标准配准方法和学习模型易收敛到度量错误局部极小值，需专用基准和配准策略。

Method: 提出RGSR（残差引导分层配准），无训练、仅几何的精化流程：通过高度分层ICP、反向配准方向和置信门控的接受-若更优选择策略，利用共享地面平面实现鲁棒配准。

Result: RGSR在主基准（9,012扫描）上达86.0% S@0.75m和99.8% S@1.0m，优于置信门控级联（83.7%）和GeoTransformer（76.3%）。验证了基于RMSE的位姿选择与独立控制及轨迹一致性，并发现极端部分重叠下Fourier-Mellin BEV提议可降低RMSE但增加实际位姿误差。

Conclusion: 提出的大规模空地LiDAR配准基准和RGSR方法有效解决了跨源模态差异问题，代码和数据集将公开。

Abstract: We introduce Paired-CSLiDAR (CSLiDAR), a cross-source aerial-ground LiDAR benchmark for single-scan pose refinement: refining a ground-scan pose within a 50 m-radius aerial crop. The benchmark contains 12,683 ground-aerial pairs across 6 evaluation sites and per-scan reference 6-DoF alignments for sub-meter root-mean-square error (RMSE) evaluation. Because aerial scans capture rooftops and canopy while ground scans capture facades and under-canopy, the two modalities share only a fraction of their geometry, primarily the terrain surface, causing standard registration methods and learned correspondence models to converge to metrically incorrect local minima. We propose Residual-Guided Stratified Registration (RGSR), a training-free, geometry-only refinement pipeline that exploits the shared ground plane through height-stratified ICP, reversed registration directions, and confidence-gated accept-if-better selection. RGSR achieves 86.0% S@0.75 m and 99.8% S@1.0 m on the primary benchmark of 9,012 scans, outperforming both the confidence-gated cascade at 83.7% and GeoTransformer at 76.3%. We validate RMSE-based pose selection with independent survey control and trajectory consistency, and show that added Fourier-Mellin BEV proposals can reduce RMSE while increasing actual pose error under extreme partial overlap. The dataset and code are being prepared for public release.

</details>


### [64] [Affordance Agent Harness: Verification-Gated Skill Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00663)
*Haojian Huang,Jiahao Shi,Yinchuan Li,Yingcong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Affordance Agent Harness，一种闭环运行系统，通过证据存储、成本控制、 episodic记忆检索和自适应路由选择，结合验证器门控与最终融合判断，在开放世界场景中实现更高效、更准确的 affordance grounding，相比固定流水线方法在准确性和成本之间取得更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有affordance grounding系统采用固定流水线组合多种技能，但无法适应每个实例的难度差异，缺乏对中间错误的定向恢复机制，且未能利用重复出现对象的经验，导致在可操作区域小、遮挡、反射和视觉模糊的开放世界场景中表现不佳。

Method: 提出Affordance Agent Harness闭环运行时系统，包含：(1) 证据存储与成本控制模块；(2)  episodic记忆检索为重复类别提供先验；(3) Router自适应选择并参数化技能；(4) Affordance-specific Verifier通过自一致性、跨尺度稳定性和证据充分性进行门控决策，触发定向重试；(5) 最终Judge融合累积证据和轨迹产生预测。

Result: 在多个affordance基准测试和难度控制子集上，相比固定流水线基线，实现了更优的准确性-成本帕累托前沿，同时提高了接地质量并减少了平均技能调用次数和延迟。

Conclusion: 通过闭环自适应推理和证据验证机制，可以有效解决开放场景中affordance grounding的系统性问题，在保证预测质量的同时显著降低计算开销。

Abstract: Affordance grounding requires identifying where and how an agent should interact in open-world scenes, where actionable regions are often small, occluded, reflective, and visually ambiguous. Recent systems therefore combine multiple skills (e.g., detection, segmentation, interaction-imagination), yet most orchestrate them with fixed pipelines that are poorly matched to per-instance difficulty, offer limited targeted recovery from intermediate errors, and fail to reuse experience from recurring objects. These failures expose a systems problem: test-time grounding must acquire the right evidence, decide whether that evidence is reliable enough to commit, and do so under bounded inference cost without access to labels. We propose Affordance Agent Harness, a closed-loop runtime that unifies heterogeneous skills with an evidence store and cost control, retrieves episodic memories to provide priors for recurring categories, and employs a Router to adaptively select and parameterize skills. An affordance-specific Verifier then gates commitments using self-consistency, cross-scale stability, and evidence sufficiency, triggering targeted retries before a final judge fuses accumulated evidence and trajectories into the prediction. Experiments on multiple affordance benchmarks and difficulty-controlled subsets show a stronger accuracy-cost Pareto frontier than fixed-pipeline baselines, improving grounding quality while reducing average skill calls and latency. Project page: https://tenplusgood.github.io/a-harness-page/.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [65] [FedACT: Concurrent Federated Intelligence across Heterogeneous Data Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00011)
*Md Sirajul Islam,Isabelle G Chapman,N I Md Ashafuddula,Xu Yuan,Li Chen,Nian-Feng Tzeng,Klara Nahrstedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedACT是一种面向多并发联邦学习任务的异构资源感知设备调度方法，通过对齐评分机制和参与公平性策略，最小化平均作业完成时间并提升模型精度，在实验中实现了最高8.3倍的JCT降低和44.5%的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习研究多聚焦于单任务优化，而实际场景中多个FL任务需同时在共享设备池上训练。直接应用单任务优化技术会导致系统性能次优，主要问题在于设备异构性和资源利用效率低下。

Method: 提出FedACT框架，核心包含：(1) 对齐评分机制，评估设备可用资源与作业资源需求的匹配度；(2) 参与公平性策略，确保设备在各作业间均衡贡献；(3) 基于对齐分数优先级的调度方案，在最小化平均JCT的同时保障公平参与。

Result: 在多样化的FL作业和基准数据集上的实验表明，相比现有最优基线方法，FedACT将平均作业完成时间降低最高8.3倍，模型精度提升最高44.5%。

Conclusion: FedACT有效解决了多并发联邦学习场景中设备异构性和资源低效问题，通过智能调度显著提升系统整体性能，同时兼顾了模型精度与完成时间。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative intelligence across decentralized data source devices in a privacy-preserving way. While substantial research attention has been drawn to optimizing the learning process for an individual task, real-world applications increasingly require multiple machine learning tasks simultaneously training their models across a shared pool of devices. Naively applying single-FL optimization techniques in multi-FL systems results in suboptimal system performance, particularly due to device heterogeneity and resource inefficiency. To address such a critical open challenge, we introduce {\em FedACT}, a novel resource heterogeneity-aware device scheduling approach designed to efficiently schedule heterogeneous devices across multiple concurrent FL jobs, with the goal of minimizing their average job completion time (JCT). {\em FedACT} dynamically assigns devices to FL jobs based on an alignment scoring mechanism that evaluates the compatibility between available resources of devices and resource demands of jobs. Additionally, it incorporates participation fairness to ensure balanced contributions from devices across jobs, further enhancing the accuracy levels of learned global models. An optimal scheduling plan is formulated in {\em FedACT} by prioritizing devices with higher alignment scores, while ensuring fair participation across jobs. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed scheduling algorithm, we carried out comprehensive experiments using diverse FL jobs and benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that {\em FedACT} reduces the average JCT by up to 8.3\(\times\) and improves model accuracy by up to 44.5\%, compared to the state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [66] [Beyond Continuity: Simulation-free Reconstruction of Discrete Branching Dynamics from Single-cell Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00545)
*Junda Ying,Yuxuan Wang,Bowen Yang,Peijie Zhou,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非平衡薛定谔桥（USB），一种无模拟的框架，用于从快照数据中学习单细胞分辨率的随机与非平衡（增殖/凋亡）动力学子轨迹，该框架解决了分支薛定谔桥问题，并在模拟和真实数据集上实现了优于或可比于确定性基线的轨迹重建性能，且能唯一实现离散出生-死亡动态的现实模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非平衡最优传输方法将细胞质量视为连续流体，仅在群体层面进行推断，无法捕捉单细胞分辨率下出生-死亡事件的离散跳跃特性，而这对于理解谱系分支和命运决定至关重要。

Method: 提出Unbalanced Schrödinger Bridge（USB）框架，提供分支薛定谔桥问题的可解方案，引入无模拟训练目标，使单个细胞同时经历布朗运动和离散出生-死亡跳跃，实现微观解释。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上，USB的轨迹重建性能优于或可比于确定性基线，并且是唯一能实现单细胞分辨率下离散出生-死亡动态现实模拟的方法。

Conclusion: USB有效融合随机效应与非平衡效应，在单细胞分辨率下建模离散跳跃式出生-死亡动力学，为理解谱系分支和命运决定提供了更精确的工具。

Abstract: Inferring cellular trajectories from destructive snapshots is complicated by the challenges of stochasticity and non-conservative mass dynamics such as cell proliferation and apoptosis. Existing unbalanced Optimal Transport (OT) methods treat mass as a continuous fluid, performing inference at the population level. However, this macroscopic view often fails to capture the discrete, jump-like nature of birth-death events at single-cell resolution, which is essential for understanding lineage branching and fate decisions. We present Unbalanced Schrödinger Bridge (USB), a simulation-free framework for learning underlying dynamics that effectively integrates both stochastic and unbalanced effects which also models the discrete, jump-like birth-death dynamics at single-cell resolution. Theoretically, USB provides a tractable solution to the Branching Schrödinger Bridge (BSB) problem, offering a rigorous microscopic interpretation where individual cells undergo both Brownian motion and discrete birth-death jumps. Technically, the method implements an efficient solver by introducing a simulation-free training objective that effectively scales to high-dimensional omics data. Empirically, we demonstrate on both simulated and real-world datasets that USB not only achieves trajectory reconstruction performance better than or comparable to deterministic baselines but also uniquely enables realistic discrete simulation of birth-death dynamics at single-cell resolution.

</details>


### [67] [What Physics do Data-Driven MoCap-to-Radar Models Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00018)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理解释性的评估框架，通过两个互补指标（物理对齐度与速度干预下的频率关系保持度）来判断MoCap-to-radar模型是否真正学习了底层物理规律。实验表明，低重构误差不代表物理一致性，而时间注意力机制对transformer模型学习物理规律至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的MoCap-to-radar模型虽能生成看似合理的微多普勒谱图，但缺乏对其是否真正学习到背后物理规律的验证方法。作者旨在回答"模型是否真正学习了底层物理规律"这一问题。

Method: 提出了基于物理解释性的评估框架，包含两个互补指标：(1) 模型预测与物理推导的多普勒频率之间的对齐度；(2) 速度干预下模型预测是否保持速度-频率关系。两个指标仅需MoCap输入和模型预测，无需真实雷达数据。

Result: 不同模型架构的实验表明，低重构误差并不能保证物理一致性：部分模型重构误差低但在两个物理指标上表现差。进一步分析发现时间注意力机制对transformer模型学习底层物理规律至关重要。

Conclusion: 单纯的生成质量评估不足以衡量模型对物理规律的理解，需要物理驱动的解释性指标；时间注意力是transformer模型捕获物理规律的关键因素。

Abstract: Data-driven MoCap-to-radar models generate plausible micro-Doppler spectrograms, but do they actually learn the underlying physics? We introduce a physics-based interpretability framework to answer this question via two proposed complementary metrics: one measures alignment between model predictions and the physics-derived Doppler frequency, while the other tests whether predictions preserve the velocity-frequency relationship under velocity intervention. Both metrics require only MoCap input and model predictions, without access to measured radar data. Experiments across several model architectures reveal that low reconstruction error does not guarantee physical consistency: some, but not all, models achieve low error yet perform poorly on the two physics-based metrics. Further analysis shows that temporal attention is critical for transformer-based models to learn the underlying physics.

</details>


### [68] [Observable Performance Does Not Fully Reflect System Organization: A Multi-Level Analysis of Gait Dynamics Under Occlusal Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00778)
*Jacques Raynal,Pierre Slangen,Jacques Margerit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，在适应性的神经力学系统中，相同的外部表现可能对应不同的内部系统状态，传统聚合指标无法区分这些差异。研究以帕金森病患者咬合垂直距离为约束条件，通过三个分析层次（聚合指标、动力学系统、潜在空间嵌入）揭示了表现与系统组织之间的解耦现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑生物力学研究中一个隐含假设：可观测的外部表现能否真实反映系统内部组织状态？尤其在自适应系统中，这一对应关系可能不成立。作者试图通过VDO这一临床可操作的约束条件，探索系统层面响应与内部状态之间的复杂关系。

Method: 采用单病例设计（帕金森病患者），在重复条件下进行组内分析。分析方法分三个层次：(i)聚合线性指标（可观测表现）；(ii)动力学系统框架（状态空间中的时间组织）；(iii)无监督嵌入获得的潜在空间表征。此外还提出了一个未实现的第四层次（概念性扩展）。

Result: 具有相当可观测表现的条件，在状态空间和潜在空间表征中可能对应不同的系统组织方式。这种解耦现象表明聚合指标的局限性，相似输出可能来源于不等价的系统状态。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种多层级分析框架，用于分析受约束驱动的系统。但强调结果是探索性的、非因果的，未建立机械性、预测性或方向性的关系，仅提供结构化的分析途径。

Abstract: In biomechanical systems, observable performance is often used as a proxy for underlying system organization. However, this assumption implicitly presumes a correspondence between output metrics and internal system states that may not hold in adaptive systems. In this study, the vertical dimension of occlusion (VDO) is considered as a constraint applied to an adaptive neuromechanical system, enabling the exploration of system-level responses under controlled variations. A single-case design in a patient with Parkinson's disease allows an intra-individual analysis across repeated conditions.The analysis is structured across three complementary levels: (i) aggregated linear metrics describing observable performance, (ii) a dynamical systems framework describing temporal organization in state space, and (iii) a latent space representation obtained through unsupervised embedding. The results show that conditions with comparable observable performance may correspond to different organizations in both state space and latent space representations. This dissociation highlights a limitation of aggregated metrics and suggests that similar outputs may arise from non-equivalent system states. A fourth level is proposed as a purely conceptual extension describing potential relationships between system states. This level is not implemented and is not derived from experimental data. These observations are strictly exploratory and non-causal. The proposed framework does not establish mechanistic, predictive, or directional relationships, but provides a structured approach for analyzing constraint-driven systems across multiple levels of representation.

</details>


### [69] [Learning physically grounded traffic accident reconstruction from public accident reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00050)
*Yanchen Guan,Haicheng Liao,Chengyue Wang,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于公开事故报告和场景测量的参数化多模态学习方法，构建了包含6,217个真实事故案例的CISS-REC数据集，开发了从报告语义到道路拓扑、参与者属性、车道一致运动重建及碰撞交互优化的重建框架，在事故点精度和碰撞一致性上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通事故通常以文本报告形式记录，但物理上准确的事故重建因详细场景测量数据和专家重建工作的稀缺、昂贵且难以规模化而面临困难。

Method: 将事故重建建模为参数化多模态学习问题；构建CISS-REC数据集（6,217个真实案例）；开发重建框架：1) 将报告语义映射到道路拓扑和参与者属性，2) 重建车道一致的事前运动，3) 通过局部几何推理和时间分配优化碰撞相关交互。

Result: 在CISS-REC数据集上，该方法优于代表性基线，实现了最强的整体重建保真度，包括改进的事故点精度和碰撞一致性。

Conclusion: 公开事故报告可作为可规模化、可定量验证的事故重建计算基础，对交通安全分析、仿真和自动驾驶研究具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Traffic accidents are routinely documented in textual reports, yet physically grounded accident reconstruction remains difficult because detailed scene measurements and expert reconstructions are scarce, costly and hard to scale. Here we formulate accident reconstruction from publicly accessible reports and scene measurements as a parameterized multimodal learning problem. We construct CISS-REC, a dataset of 6,217 real-world accident cases curated from the NHTSA Crash Investigation Sampling System, and develop a reconstruction framework that grounds report semantics to road topology and participant attributes, reconstructs lane consistent pre-impact motion, and refines collision relevant interactions through localized geometric reasoning and temporal allocation. Our method outperforms representative baselines on CISS-REC, achieving the strongest overall reconstruction fidelity, including improved accident point accuracy and collision consistency. These results show that public accident reports can serve as scalable computational substrates for quantitatively verifiable accident reconstruction, with potential value for traffic safety analysis, simulation and autonomous driving research.

</details>


### [70] [Smart Ensemble Learning Framework for Predicting Groundwater Heavy Metal Pollution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00056)
*T. Ansah-Narh,G. Y. Afrifa,J. B. Tandoh,K. Asare,M. Addi,K. E. Yorke,D. M. A. Akpoley,K. Aidoo,S. K. Fosuhene*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对Densu盆地地下水重金属污染指数（HPI）的统计复杂性和空间异质性，构建了集成响应变换与嵌套交叉验证集成机器学习的预测框架。比较了原始、对数和高斯Copula三种变换方法在六种学习器上的表现。高斯Copula变换结合Stacked Lasso集成模型效果最佳（R²=0.96, RMSE=0.19），且残差更优、空间分布图更合理。DBSCAN聚类揭示Fe和Mn是HPI的主要贡献者。研究局限包括依赖随机交叉验证而非空间交叉验证，以及局限于单一盆地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法无法捕捉地下水重金属污染指标的统计复杂性和空间异质性，且HPI通常呈偏态分布并受相关污染物影响，导致未经变换的预测存在偏差。亟需开发能够处理非正态分布和多污染物相关性的稳健预测框架。

Method: 构建集成响应变换（原始、对数、高斯Copula）与嵌套交叉验证的集成机器学习框架。采用六种学习器：支持向量回归、k近邻、CART、Elastic Net、核岭回归及Stacked Lasso集成。使用DBSCAN聚类进行污染源解析。

Result: 原始尺度模型存在过度乐观现象（Elastic Net和Stacked集成R²≈1.0）。对数变换稳定了方差（SVM: R²=0.93, RMSE=0.18）。高斯Copula效果最佳：Stacked集成R²=0.96, RMSE=0.19。DBSCAN识别Fe和Mn为主要HPI贡献因子。

Conclusion: 分布感知的集成学习结合聚类诊断能够为地下水污染评估提供稳健且可解释的结果。未来应探索空间验证方法并推广至其他地质环境。

Abstract: Groundwater in the Densu Basin is increasingly threatened by heavy metal contamination, but conventional methods fail to capture the statistical complexity and spatial heterogeneity of pollution indicators. A key challenge is modelling the Heavy Metal Pollution Index (HPI), which is typically skewed and affected by correlated contaminants, leading to biased predictions without transformation. This study develops a predictive framework integrating response transformations with nested cross-validated ensemble machine learning. Three transformations (raw, log, and Gaussian copula) were applied to HPI and evaluated across six learners: support vector regression (SVM), $k$-nearest neighbours (k-NN), CART, Elastic Net, kernel ridge regression, and a stacked Lasso ensemble. Raw-scale models produced deceptively high fits (Elastic Net and stacked ensemble $R^2 \approx 1.0$), suggesting over-optimism. The log transformation stabilised variance (SVM: $R^2 = 0.93$, RMSE $= 0.18$; k-NN: $R^2 = 0.92$, RMSE $= 0.20$). The Gaussian copula gave the most reliable results: stacked ensemble $R^2 = 0.96$ (RMSE $= 0.19$), with other learners maintaining high accuracy. Copula-based models improved residuals and produced spatially plausible maps. DBSCAN clustering revealed Fe and Mn as primary HPI contributors, consistent with regional hydrogeochemistry. Limitations include reliance on random (not spatial) cross-validation and basin-specific scope. Future work should explore spatial validation and other geological settings. Overall, distribution-aware ensembles with clustering diagnostics offer robust, interpretable assessments of groundwater contamination.

</details>


### [71] [Human-in-the-Loop Meta Bayesian Optimization for Fusion Energy and Scientific Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00068)
*Ricardo Luna Gutierrez,Sahand Ghorbanpour,Ejaz Rahman,Varchas Gopalaswamy,Riccardo Betti,Vineet Gundecha,Aarne Lees,Soumyendu Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HL-MBO框架，通过将专家知识与元学习贝叶斯优化相结合，在数据稀缺的高风险科学领域（如惯性约束聚变、分子优化、超导材料）中加速实验发现，并优于现有贝叶斯优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惯性约束聚变（ICF）虽有望提供可持续的清洁能源，但受限于高昂成本和有限的实验机会，传统方法难以在数据稀缺条件下高效优化。

Method: 提出人机协同元贝叶斯优化（HL-MBO）框架，包含元学习的替代模型和融合专家知识的采集函数，可推荐候选实验并提供可解释的决策说明。

Result: HL-MBO在ICF能量产额优化、分子优化及超导材料临界温度最大化等基准任务上均优于当前贝叶斯优化方法。

Conclusion: 融合专家知识与不确定性感知的机器学习框架能够有效加速数据稀缺、高风险科学领域的发现进程，兼具性能优势与可解释性。

Abstract: Inertial Confinement Fusion (ICF) holds transformative promise for sustainable, near-limitless clean energy, yet remains constrained by prohibitively high costs and limited experimental opportunities. This paper presents Human-in-the-Loop Meta Bayesian Optimization (HL-MBO), a framework that integrates expert knowledge with few-shot, uncertainty-aware machine learning to accelerate discovery in data-scarce, high-stakes scientific domains.
